2 Termination Fee 77 -vi- AGREEMENT AND PLAN OF MERGER THIS AGREEMENT AND PLAN OF MERGER, dated as of November 1, 2026 (this “ Agreement ”), by and among Marvell Technology, Inc., a Delaware corporation (“ Parent ”), Java Merger Sub, Inc., a Delaware corporation (“ Merger Sub I ”), Java Merger Sub II, Inc., a Delaware corporation (“ Merger Sub II ” and together with Merger Sub I, each a “ Merger Sub ” and collectively the “ Merger Subs ” ), and Lumentum Holdings Inc., a Delaware corporation (the “ Company ”). W I T N E S S E T H: WHEREAS, it is proposed that Merger Sub I shall merge with and into the Company, with the Company surviving the merger as a wholly owned subsidiary of Parent (the “ First Merger ”), upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement and in accordance with the applicable provisions of the DGCL, pursuant to which the Company Common Stock, the Company Series A Preferred Stock and the Company Equity Awards will be converted into the right to receive the applicable consideration in the manner set forth herein; WHEREAS, promptly following the Effective Time, the Surviving Corporation shall merge with and into Merger Sub II, with Merger Sub II surviving the merger as a wholly-owned subsidiary of Parent in accordance with the applicable provisions of the DGCL (the “ Second Merger ” and, collectively or in seriatim with the First Merger, the “ Merger ”), in each case upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement; WHEREAS, the Board of Directors of Parent has unanimously (a) approved and declared advisable this Agreement and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including the Merger, the Parent Charter Amendment and the Parent Share Issuance, on the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement, (b) determined that this Agreement and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including the Merger, the Parent Charter Amendment and the Parent Share Issuance, are fair to, and in the best interests of, Parent and the stockholders of Parent, (c) resolved to recommend the approval of the Parent Charter Amendment and the Parent Share Issuance to the stockholders of Parent, and (d) directed that approval of the Parent Charter Amendment and the Parent Share Issuance be submitted to the stockholders of Parent for approval; WHEREAS, the Board of Directors of the Company has unanimously (a) approved and declared advisable this Agreement and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including the Merger, on the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement, (b) determined that this Agreement and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including the Merger, are fair to, and in the best interests of, the Company and the stockholders of the Company, (c) resolved to recommend the adoption of this Agreement to the stockholders of the Company, and (d) directed that this Agreement be submitted to the stockholders of the Company for adoption; WHEREAS, the Board of Directors of each of Merger Sub I and Merger Sub II has (a) approved and declared advisable this Agreement and the transactions contemplated by this Agreement, including the Merger, on the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement, (b) resolved to recommend that Parent, as the sole stockholder of such Merger Sub, adopt this Agreement and (c) directed that this Agreement be submitted to Parent for adoption; and WHEREAS, the parties to this Agreement intend (i) that the First Merger and the Second Merger, taken together, constitute an integrated plan and qualify as a single “reorganization” within the meaning of Section 368(a) of the Code and (ii) for this Agreement to be, and hereby adopt this Agreement as, a “plan of reorganization” within the meaning of Treasury Regulations Section 1.368-2(g). NOW, THEREFORE, in consideration of the representations, warranties, covenants and agreements set forth herein, the parties agree as follows: ARTICLE I FORMATION; THE MERGER Section 1.1. The Merger . (a) The First Merger . Upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement and in accordance with the General Corporation Law of the State of Delaware (the “ DGCL ”), Merger Sub I shall be merged with and into the Company at the Effective Time. Upon the Effective Time, the separate corporate existence of Merger Sub I shall cease, and the Company shall continue as the surviving corporation in the First Merger (sometimes referred to herein as the “ Surviving Corporation ”) as a wholly owned subsidiary of Parent and shall succeed to and assume all the rights, privileges, immunities, properties, powers and franchises of Merger Sub I in accordance with the DGCL. (b) The Second Merger . Upon the terms and subject to the conditions set forth in this Agreement, and in accordance with the DGCL, promptly following the Effective Time, the Surviving Corporation shall be merged with and into Merger Sub II at the Second Effective Time. Upon the Second Effective Time, the separate existence of the Surviving Corporation shall cease and Merger Sub II shall continue as the surviving corporation of the Second Merger (sometimes referred to herein as the “ Second Surviving Corporation ”) and shall succeed to and assume all the rights and obligations of the Surviving Corporation in accordance with the DGCL. Section 1.2. Closing . The closing of the Merger (the “ Closing ”) shall take place at 5:00 a.m. Pacific Time, remotely via the electronic exchange of documents, on a day that is no later than the third (3rd) business day after satisfaction or permitted waiver of all of the conditions set forth in Article VII (other than those conditions that by their terms are to be fulfilled at the Closing, but subject to the fulfillment or permitted waiver of such conditions); provided , that the Closing may occur at such other time, date or place as may be agreed to in writing by the parties hereto (the date on which the Closing occurs, the “ Closing Date ”). Section 1.3. Effective Times . (a) Effective Time . Subject to the provisions of this Agreement, as soon as practicable on the Closing Date, the parties shall cause the First Merger to be consummated by filing with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware a Certificate of Merger (the “ First Certificate of Merger ”) with respect to the First Merger, duly executed and completed in accordance with the relevant provisions of the DGCL, and shall make all other filings or recordings required under the DGCL with respect to the First Merger. The First Merger shall become effective at the time when the First Certificate of Merger has been duly filed with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware or such later time as may be mutually agreed by the parties and specified in the First Certificate of Merger (the “ Effective Time ”). (b) Second Effective Time . Subject to the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement, promptly following the Effective Time, the parties shall cause the Second Merger to be consummated by filing with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware a Certificate of Merger (the “ Second Certificate of Merger ” and, together with the First Certificate of Merger, the “ Certificates of Merger ”) with respect to the Second Merger, duly executed and completed in accordance with the relevant provisions of the DGCL, and shall make all other filings or recordings required under the DGCL with respect to the Second Merger. The Second Merger shall become effective at the time when the Second Certificate of Merger has been duly filed with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware or such later time as may be mutually agreed by the parties and specified in the Second Certificate of Merger (the “ Second Effective Time ”). -2- Section 1.4. Effects of the Transaction . The Merger shall have the effects set forth in this Agreement and the applicable provisions of the DGCL. ARTICLE II CERTAIN GOVERNANCE MATTERS Section 2.1. Organizational Documents; Board of Directors of Parent . (a) Certificate of Incorporation of Parent . Subject to the approval of the Certificate of Amendment attached hereto as Exhibit A (the “ Parent Charter Amendment ”) by the affirmative vote of a majority of the votes cast by the holders of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series A Preferred Stock, voting together as a single class (with the shares of Parent Series A Preferred Stock voting on an as-converted basis), at the Parent Stockholders Meeting (the “ Parent Charter Amendment Approval ”), the filing of the Parent Charter Amendment, and, if applicable, the filing of the Parent Series B Certificate of Designation, with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware, the Certificate of Incorporation of Parent, as amended by the Parent Charter Amendment, and, if applicable, the Parent Series B Certificate of Designation, shall be the Certificate of Incorporation of Parent following the Effective Time until thereafter amended in accordance with its respective terms and with Applicable Law. (b) Bylaws of Parent . The Bylaws of Parent as in effect immediately prior to the Effective Time shall continue to be the Bylaws of Parent following the Effective Time until thereafter amended in accordance with its respective terms and with Applicable Law. (c) Board Designees . Prior to the Closing Date, the Company shall designate three (3) individuals reasonably acceptable to Parent (such acceptance not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed) (the “ Company Board Designees ”), who shall be selected from among the members of the Board of Directors of the Company as of the date of this Agreement; provided that such Company Board Designees shall meet the criteria for service on the Board of Directors of Parent under Applicable Law (including Nasdaq rules), and any bona fide criteria established prior to the date hereof by the Board of Directors of Parent or the Nominating and Governance Committee of the Board of Directors of Parent for such service that are generally applicable to members of the Board of Directors of Parent. Prior to the Closing, Parent shall take all necessary actions to cause the Company Board Designees to be appointed to the Board of Directors of Parent effective as of immediately following the Effective Time, including by increasing the size of the Board of Directors of Parent as may be necessary in connection therewith. Each of the Company Board Designees shall hold office until the earliest to occur of the appointment or election and qualification of his or her respective successor or his or her death, resignation, disqualification or proper removal as a member of Parent’s Board of Directors in accordance with Parent’s Certificate of Incorporation and Bylaws. Parent shall cause each Company Board Designee who is at the time still serving as a member of the Company’s Board of Directors to be nominated for election at the first annual meeting of Parent’s stockholders following the Effective Time (subject to continued satisfaction of generally applicable criteria for service on the Board of Directors of Parent and unless such Company Board Designee is unable or unwilling to serve). -3- Section 2.2. Organizational Documents; Directors and Officers of the Surviving Corporation and the Second Surviving Corporation . (a) At the Effective Time, without limiting Section 6.4(a) , (i) the Certificate of Incorporation of the Surviving Corporation shall be amended and restated in its entirety in the form set forth on Exhibit B , which will be the Certificate of Incorporation of the Surviving Corporation until thereafter amended in accordance with its terms and Applicable Law and (ii) the Bylaws of the Surviving Corporation shall be amended and restated in their entirety to be in the form of the Bylaws of Merger Sub I set forth on Exhibit C , as in effect immediately prior to the Effective Time (except that references to Merger Sub I’s name shall be replaced with “Lumentum Holdings Inc.”) until thereafter changed or amended in accordance with their terms and with Applicable Law. (b) The directors and officers of Merger Sub I immediately prior to the Effective Time shall be the directors and officers, respectively, of the Surviving Corporation, in each case until such individual’s successor is elected and qualified or such individual’s earlier death, resignation or removal, in each case in accordance with the Bylaws of the Surviving Corporation. (c) At the Second Effective Time, (i) the Certificate of Incorporation of Merger Sub II as in effect immediately prior to the Second Effective Time shall be the Certificate of Incorporation of the Second Surviving Corporation, as amended at the Second Effective Time to change the name of the Second Surviving Corporation to “Lumentum Holdings Inc.”, until thereafter amended in accordance with its terms and Applicable Law; and (ii) the Bylaws of Merger Sub II as in effect immediately prior to the Second Effective Time shall be the Bylaws of the Second Surviving Corporation until thereafter changed or amended in accordance with its terms and with Applicable Law. (d) The board of directors and officers of Merger Sub II immediately prior to the Second Effective Time shall be the board of directors and officers, respectively, of the Second Surviving Corporation, in each case until such individual’s successor is elected or appointed or such individual’s earlier death, resignation or removal, in each case in accordance with the Bylaws of the Second Surviving Corporation. ARTICLE III EFFECT OF THE MERGER ON THE CAPITAL STOCK OF THE COMPANY; EXCHANGE OF CERTIFICATES Section 3.1. Effect on Capital Stock of the Company . (a) Conversion of Company Common Stock, Company Preferred Stock and Merger Sub I Common Stock . As of the Effective Time, by virtue of the First Merger, and without any action on the part of the Company, Parent, the Merger Subs or the holders of any securities of the Company, Parent or the Merger Subs: (i) Each share of Company Common Stock that is issued and outstanding as of immediately prior to the Effective Time (other than any shares of Company Common Stock to be canceled pursuant to Section 3.1(a)(iii)) shall be converted into the right to receive 4.50 fully paid and nonassessable shares of Parent Common Stock (the “ Exchange Ratio ”) (and, if applicable, cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable in accordance with Section 3.1(e) ) (the “ Common Stock Merger Consideration ”). As of the Effective Time, all such shares of Company Common Stock shall no longer be outstanding and shall automatically be canceled and retired and shall cease to exist. As of the Effective Time, each holder of a Certificate or Book-Entry Share representing any shares of Company Common Stock shall cease to have any rights with respect thereto, except the right to receive the Common Stock Merger Consideration in accordance with Section 3.2 . -4- (ii) Each share of Company Series A Preferred Stock that is issued and outstanding immediately prior to the Effective Time (other than Dissenting Shares and any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock to be canceled pursuant to Section 3.1(a)(iii) ) shall be converted into the right to receive a number of fully paid and nonassessable shares of Parent Common Stock equal to the product of (A) the number of shares of Company Common Stock into which such share of Company Series A Preferred Stock is convertible pursuant to the terms of the Company Certificate of Designation as of immediately prior to the Effective Time, without regard to any limitations on conversion set forth therein or otherwise, and (B) the Exchange Ratio (and, if applicable, cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable in accordance with Section 3.1(e) ) (the “ Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration ”); provided , however , that if the holder of any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock has delivered an irrevocable election in writing to the Company, with a copy to Parent, on or prior to the date that is at least ten business days prior to the date of the Company Stockholders Meeting, (1) affirmatively electing to receive the Merger Consideration in respect of all such shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock in the form of Parent Series B Preferred Stock; (2) waiving its right to receive the Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration in respect of all such shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock and (3) waiving its right to appraisal under Section 262 of the DGCL with respect to its shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock, together with any instruments, waivers, consents and other documentation as may reasonably be requested by Parent or the Company in connection therewith (a “ Preferred Stock Election ”), then each share of Company Series A Preferred Stock that is issued and outstanding and held by such holder making a Preferred Stock Election immediately prior to the Effective Time (other than Dissenting Shares and any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock to be canceled pursuant to Section 3.1(a)(iii) ) shall be converted into the right to receive one fully paid and nonassessable share of Parent Series B Preferred Stock, with terms set forth in the Parent Series B Certificate of Designation (the “ Series B Preferred Stock Merger Consideration ” and, together with the Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration, the “ Preferred Stock Merger Consideration ” and such Preferred Stock Merger Consideration, as applicable, collectively with the Common Stock Merger Consideration, the “ Merger Consideration ”). For clarity, (x) the Series B Preferred Stock Merger Consideration shall be issued in lieu of the Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration with respect to any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock that are the subject of a Preferred Stock Election; (y) if a holder of Company Series A Preferred Stock has not made a timely and valid Preferred Stock Election, such holder shall be entitled to receive only the Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration with respect to any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock issued and outstanding and held by such holder as of immediately prior to the Effective Time, subject to Section 3.1(f)(ii) and (z) a holder of Company Series A Preferred Stock may not make a Preferred Stock Election with respect to less than all of such holder’s shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock. As of the Effective Time, all such shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock shall no longer be outstanding and shall automatically be canceled and retired and shall cease to exist. As of the Effective Time, each holder of a Certificate or Book-Entry Share representing any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock shall cease to have any rights with respect thereto, except the right to receive the applicable Preferred Stock Merger Consideration in accordance with Section 3.2 (or, in the case of Dissenting Shares, the rights set forth in Section 3.1(f)(ii) ). (iii) Each share of Company Capital Stock that is immediately prior to the Effective Time (A) held in the treasury of the Company or (B) held directly by Parent or the Merger Subs, in each case other than shares of Company Common Stock held in trust accounts, managed accounts, mutual funds and the like, or otherwise held in a fiduciary or agency capacity that are beneficially owned by third parties, shall automatically be canceled and retired and shall cease to exist, and no consideration shall be delivered in exchange therefor. -5- (iv) Each share of common stock of Merger Sub I issued and outstanding immediately prior to the Effective Time shall be converted into one fully paid and nonassessable share of common stock of the Surviving Corporation. (b) At the Second Effective Time, by virtue of the Second Merger and without any action on the part of Parent, either Merger Sub, the Company or any holder of common stock of the Surviving Corporation or common stock of Merger Sub II, (i) each share of common stock of Merger Sub II issued and outstanding immediately prior to the Second Effective Time shall remain outstanding as a share of common stock of the Second Surviving Corporation and shall not be affected by the Second Merger and (ii) each share of common stock of the Surviving Corporation issued and outstanding immediately prior to the Second Effective Time shall be cancelled and shall cease to exist, and no consideration shall be paid with respect thereto, such that, immediately following the Second Merger, the Second Surviving Corporation shall be a direct wholly owned subsidiary of Parent. (c) Treatment of Company Equity Awards . (i) The Merger shall constitute a “change in control” or “corporate transaction,” as applicable, for purposes of the Company Equity Plans and all awards granted thereunder. (ii) Each option to purchase shares of Company Common Stock granted under a Company Equity Plan (each, a “ Company Option ”), whether vested or unvested, that is outstanding as of immediately prior to the Effective Time shall, as of the Effective Time, automatically and without any action on the part of the holder thereof be assumed by Parent and converted into an option to acquire, on the same terms and conditions as were applicable under such Company Option immediately prior to the Effective Time (including with respect to vesting, termination protection and provisions, expiration and post-termination exercisability), a number of shares of Parent Common Stock (rounded down to the nearest whole share) equal to the product of (1) the number of shares of Company Common Stock subject to such Company Option immediately prior to the Effective Time and (2) the Exchange Ratio, at an exercise price per share of Parent Common Stock (rounded up to the nearest whole cent) equal to the quotient of (x) the exercise price per share of Company Common Stock of such Company Option immediately prior to the Effective Time divided by (y) the Exchange Ratio (each such option, a “ Converted Option ”). The adjustments provided in this Section 3.1(c)(ii) with respect to Company Options, whether or not such Company Options are “incentive stock options” (as defined in Section 422 of the Code), are intended to be effected in a manner that is consistent with Sections 409A and 424(a) of the Code. (iii) Each award of restricted stock units relating to Company Common Stock granted under a Company Equity Plan and subject to time-based vesting conditions but not subject to performance-based vesting conditions (each, a “ Company RSU Award ”) that is outstanding and held by a non-employee member of the Board of Directors of the Company as of immediately prior to the Effective Time (each, an “ Accelerated RSU Award ”) shall, as of the Effective Time, automatically and without any action on the part of the holder thereof, be cancelled and converted into the right to receive a number of shares of Parent Common Stock equal to the product, rounded to the nearest whole number of shares, of (1) the number of shares of Company Common Stock subject to such Accelerated RSU Award immediately prior to the Effective Time and (2) the Exchange Ratio. -6- (iv) Each Company RSU Award that is outstanding as of immediately prior to the Effective Time (including any Company RSU Award that was subject to performance-based vesting conditions that were satisfied prior to the Effective Time) and is not an Accelerated RSU Award (each, a “ Non-Accelerated RSU Award ”) shall, as of the Effective Time, automatically and without any action on the part of the holder thereof, be assumed by Parent and converted into an award of Parent restricted stock units (each, a “ Converted RSU Award ”) with the same terms and conditions that applied to such Non-Accelerated RSU Award immediately prior to the Effective Time (including with respect to vesting and termination protection and provisions), relating to a number of shares of Parent Common Stock equal to the product, rounded to the nearest whole number of shares, of (1) the number of shares of Company Common Stock subject to such Company RSU Award immediately prior to the Effective Time and (2) the Exchange Ratio. (v) Each award of restricted stock units relating to Company Common Stock granted under a Company Equity Plan and subject to performance-based vesting conditions (each, a “ Company PSU Award ”) that is outstanding and for which the applicable performance period is in effect as of immediately prior to the Effective Time shall, as of the Effective Time, automatically and without any action on the part of the holder thereof be assumed by Parent and converted into an award of Parent restricted stock units (each, a “ Converted PSU Award ”) with the same terms and conditions that applied to such Company PSU Award immediately prior to the Effective Time (including with respect to vesting and termination protection and provisions and other than terms and conditions relating to the achievement of applicable performance-based vesting conditions), relating to a number of shares of Parent Common Stock equal to the product, rounded to the nearest whole number of shares, of (1) the number of shares of Company Common Stock subject to such Company PSU Award immediately prior to the Effective Time (with such number of shares determined by deeming the applicable performance conditions to be achieved (A) at the greater of target and actual levels of performance with respect to any performance conditions related to stock price performance and (B) except as otherwise provided by the terms of the Company Benefit Plans, including those listed on Section 3.1(c)(v) of the Company Disclosure Letter, that would apply assuming a participant’s qualifying termination of employment upon the Closing, at actual levels of performance for all performance conditions other than those relating to stock price performance, in either case with actual performance determined as of the latest practicable date prior to the Effective Time based upon a good faith estimate of projected full-period results by the Compensation Committee of the Board of Directors of the Company in good faith after consultation with Parent) and (2) the Exchange Ratio. (vi) Prior to the Effective Time, the Board of Directors of the Company or the appropriate committee thereof shall take all actions necessary to effect the treatment of the Company Options, the Company RSU Awards, and the Company PSU Awards (collectively, the “ Company Equity Awards ”) as contemplated by this Section 3.1(c) . (vii) As soon as practicable after the Effective Time, Parent shall prepare and file with the SEC a registration statement on Form S-8 (or other applicable form) registering the shares of Parent Common Stock necessary to fulfill Parent’s obligations under this Section 3.1(c) . Parent shall take all corporate action necessary to reserve for issuance a sufficient number of shares of Parent Common Stock for delivery with respect to the Company Equity Awards assumed by it in accordance with this Section 3.1(c) . (d) Company ESPP . Prior to the Effective Time, the Company will take all actions reasonably necessary (i) to terminate the Company ESPP as of immediately prior to the Effective Time, (ii) to provide that the “Offering Period” (as defined in the Company ESPP) that would otherwise be in progress on the Closing Date, if any, will be shortened in accordance with the terms of the Company -7- ESPP, and the last day of each such Offering Period shall be at least three (3) business days prior to the Closing Date (the “ Final Purchase Date ”), with purchases made on the Final Purchase Date contingent upon the occurrence of the Effective Time, and (iii) so that no new Offering Period under the Company ESPP is commenced at or after the Final Purchase Date and prior to the earlier of the termination of this Agreement or the Effective Time. Any shares of Company Common Stock acquired under the Company ESPP prior to or on the Final Purchase Date will be treated as outstanding Company Common Stock for purposes of this Section 3.1 . (e) Fractional Shares . No fractional share of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock (or certificate or scrip representing the same) shall be issued upon the conversion or surrender for exchange of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares hereunder, and such fractional share interests shall not entitle the owner thereof to any Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock, or to vote or to any other rights of a holder of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock. Each holder of shares of Company Capital Stock converted pursuant to the Merger who would otherwise have been entitled to receive a fraction of a share of Parent Common Stock (after aggregating all Certificates and Book-Entry Shares surrendered by such holder in accordance with this Article III ) (other than shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock for which a Preferred Stock Election has been timely and validly made), shall receive, in lieu thereof, cash (without interest) in an amount equal to such fraction as provided in this Section 3.1(e) . As promptly as practicable following the Effective Time, and in any event within 10 business days thereof, the Exchange Agent shall determine the excess of (i) the number of full shares of Parent Common Stock delivered to the Exchange Agent by Parent for issuance to holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares over (ii) the aggregate number of full shares of Parent Common Stock to be distributed to holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares (i.e., without including the fractional shares contemplated by the first sentence of this Section 3.1(e) ) (such excess being herein referred to as the “ Excess Shares ”). As soon as practicable after the Effective Time, and in any event within 10 business days thereof the Exchange Agent, as agent for such holders of Certificates and Book-Entry Shares, shall sell the Excess Shares at then prevailing prices on Nasdaq. The sale of the Excess Shares by the Exchange Agent shall be executed on Nasdaq and shall be executed in round lots to the extent practicable. Until the net proceeds of any such sale or sales have been distributed to the holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares, the Exchange Agent shall hold such proceeds in trust for such holders. The net proceeds of any such sale or sales of Excess Shares to be distributed to the holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares shall be reduced by any and all commissions, transfer taxes and other out-of-pocket transaction costs, as well as any expenses, of the Exchange Agent incurred in connection with such sale or sales. The Exchange Agent shall determine the portion of such net proceeds to which each holder of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares shall be entitled, if any, by multiplying the amount of the aggregate net proceeds by a fraction, the numerator of which is the amount of the fractional share interest to which such holder of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares is entitled (after aggregating all Certificates and Book-Entry Shares surrendered by such holder in accordance with this Article III ) and the denominator of which is the aggregate amount of fractional share interests to which all holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares are entitled. As soon as practicable after the determination of the amount of cash, if any, to be paid to holders of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares with respect to any fractional share interests, the Exchange Agent shall promptly pay such amounts to such holders subject to and in accordance with Section 3.2 ; provided that no cash payment in lieu of fractional shares shall be paid to any such holder pursuant to this Section 3.1(e) until the surrender of such Certificates or Book-Entry Shares in accordance with this Article III ; provided , further that no cash payment in lieu of fractional shares shall be paid with respect to any shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock for which a Preferred Stock Election has been timely and validly made. The payment of cash in lieu of fractional share interests pursuant to this Section 3.1(e) is not separately bargained-for consideration. -8- (f) Appraisal Rights and Dissenting Shares . (i) Appraisal Rights . In accordance with Section 262 of the DGCL, no appraisal rights shall be available to holders of Company Common Stock in connection with the Merger. (ii) Dissenting Shares . Notwithstanding any other provision of this Agreement to the contrary, shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock issued and outstanding immediately prior to the Effective Time and held by a holder or owned by a person who has properly exercised and perfected his or her demand for appraisal rights under Section 262 of the DGCL (the “ Dissenting Shares ”), shall not be converted into the right to receive the Preferred Stock Merger Consideration. At the Effective Time, the Dissenting Shares shall no longer be outstanding and shall automatically be canceled and shall cease to exist, and each holder or owner, as applicable, of Dissenting Shares shall cease to have any rights with respect thereto, except for the right to receive such consideration as shall be determined pursuant to Section 262 of the DGCL; provided , however , that if any such holder or owner, as applicable, shall have failed to perfect or shall have effectively withdrawn or lost its right to appraisal and payment under the DGCL (whether occurring before, at or after the Effective Time), such shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock shall thereupon be deemed to have been converted as of the Effective Time into the right to receive the Preferred Common Stock Merger Consideration, without any interest thereon, and such shares shall not be deemed to be Dissenting Shares. The Company shall give Parent (A) prompt written notice of any demands for appraisal, attempted withdrawals of such demands and any other instruments served pursuant to Section 262 of the DGCL and received by the Company and (B) the opportunity to participate in all negotiations and proceedings with respect to such demands. The Company shall not, except with the prior written consent of Parent, voluntarily make any payment with respect to, or settle or offer to settle, any such demands, or agree or commit to do any of the foregoing. The Company shall deliver a notice of appraisal rights to the holders of Company Series A Preferred Stock in accordance with Section 262(d)(1) of the DGCL. Section 3.2. Exchange of Shares and Certificates . (a) Designation of Exchange Agent; Deposit of Exchange Fund . Prior to the Closing, Parent shall enter into a customary exchange agreement (reasonably acceptable to the Company) with the transfer agent of Parent, the transfer agent of the Company or another nationally recognized financial institution or trust company designated by Parent and reasonably acceptable to the Company (the “ Exchange Agent ”) for the payment of the shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any) pursuant to Section 3.1(a) and Section 3.1(c)(iii) . At or prior to the Effective Time, Parent shall deposit or cause to be deposited with the Exchange Agent, for exchange in accordance with this Article III through the Exchange Agent, Book-Entry Shares representing the full number of whole shares of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock, as applicable, issuable pursuant to Section 3.1(a) and Section 3.1(c)(iii) , and Parent shall, after the Effective Time on the appropriate payment date, if applicable, provide or cause to be provided to the Exchange Agent any dividends or other distributions payable on such shares of Parent Common Stock pursuant to Section 3.2(d) (such shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any) provided to the Exchange Agent, together with any such dividends or other distributions with respect thereto, the “ Exchange Fund ”). (b) Letters of Transmittal and Instructions for Surrender . As promptly as practicable following the Effective Time (and in any event within five (5) business days thereafter), Parent shall cause the Exchange Agent to send to each holder of record of a certificate (a “ Certificate ”), if any, that immediately prior to the Effective Time represented outstanding shares of Company Capital Stock (i) a letter of transmittal (which shall specify that delivery of Certificates shall be effected, and risk of loss and -9- title to the Certificates shall pass only upon proper delivery of the Certificates (or affidavits of loss in lieu thereof) to the Exchange Agent, and which shall be in the form and have such other provisions as are reasonably acceptable to Parent and the Company) and (ii) instructions in customary form for use in effecting the surrender of the Certificates in exchange for (A) the number of shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any) (which shall be in book-entry form) representing, in the aggregate, the whole number of shares that such holder has the right to receive in respect of such Certificates pursuant to Section 3.1(a) , (B) any dividends or other distributions payable pursuant to Section 3.2(d) and (C) cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable pursuant to Section 3.1(e) . (c) Exchange Procedures . Upon surrender of a Certificate (or affidavit of loss in lieu thereof) for cancellation to the Exchange Agent, together with a letter of transmittal duly completed and validly executed in accordance with the instructions thereto, and such other documents as may be required pursuant to such instructions, the holder of such Certificate shall be entitled to receive in exchange therefor (i) the number of shares of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock (which shall be in book-entry form) representing, in the aggregate, the whole number of shares that such holder has the right to receive in respect of such Certificate pursuant to Section 3.1(a) , (ii) any dividends or other distributions payable pursuant to Section 3.2(d) and (iii) any cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable pursuant to Section 3.1(e) , and the Certificate so surrendered shall be forthwith canceled. No holder of record of a book-entry share (a “ Book-Entry Share ”) that immediately prior to the Effective Time represented outstanding shares of Company Capital Stock or any holder of an Accelerated RSU Award shall be required to deliver a Certificate or letter of transmittal for such Book-Entry Shares or Accelerated RSU Awards, and in lieu thereof, each Book-Entry Share shall, upon receipt of an “agent’s message” in customary form or such other customary evidence, if any, as the Exchange Agent may reasonably request (it being understood that the holders of Book-Entry Shares will be deemed to have surrendered such Book-Entry Shares upon receipt of such “agent’s message” or other evidence), be entitled to receive in exchange therefor (x) the number of shares of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock (which shall be in book-entry form) representing, in the aggregate, the whole number of shares that such holder has the right to receive in respect of such Book-Entry Shares pursuant to Section 3.1(a) or Section 3.1(c)(iii), as applicable, (y) any dividends or other distributions payable pursuant to Section 3.2(d) and (z) any cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable pursuant to Section 3.1(e) . Until surrendered, in each case, as contemplated by this Section 3.2(c) , each Certificate or Book-Entry Share shall be deemed, from and after the Effective Time, to represent only the right to receive the Merger Consideration as contemplated by this Section 3.2(c) and any dividends or other distributions payable pursuant to Section 3.2(d) . The Exchange Agent shall accept such Certificates (or affidavits of loss in lieu thereof) and make such payments and deliveries with respect to such surrendered Certificates, Book-Entry Shares and Accelerated RSU Awards upon compliance with such reasonable terms and conditions as the Exchange Agent may impose to effect an orderly exchange thereof in accordance with normal exchange practices. No interest shall be paid or accrued for the benefit of holders of the Certificates, Book-Entry Shares or Accelerated RSU Awards on the Merger Consideration or any cash payable hereunder. (d) Distributions with Respect to Unexchanged Shares . No dividends or other distributions with respect to Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock with a record date after the Effective Time shall be paid to the holder of any unsurrendered Certificate or Book-Entry Share with respect to any Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock represented thereby, and no cash payment in lieu of fractional shares shall be paid to any such holder pursuant to Section 3.1(e) , in each case, until the surrender of such Certificate or Book-Entry Share in accordance with this Article III . Subject to the effect of Applicable Laws, following surrender of any such Certificate or Book-Entry Share, there shall be paid to the holders of the Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock issued in exchange for Certificates, or Book-Entry Shares, or Accelerated RSU Awards pursuant to -10- Section 3.2(c) , without interest, (i) at the time of delivery of such Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock by the Exchange Agent pursuant to Section 3.2(c) , the amount of dividends or other distributions, if any, with a record date after the Effective Time theretofore paid with respect to such shares of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock, and (ii) at the appropriate payment date, the amount of dividends or other distributions, if any, with a record date after the Effective Time, but prior to such delivery of such Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock by the Exchange Agent pursuant to Section 3.2(c) , and a payment date subsequent to such delivery of such Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any) by the Exchange Agent pursuant to Section 3.2(c) , payable with respect to such shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any). (e) Certain Transfers of Ownership . In the event of a transfer of ownership of Company Capital Stock that is not registered in the transfer records of the Company, payment of the appropriate amount of Merger Consideration (and any dividends or other distributions with respect to Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock as contemplated by Section 3.2(d) ) may be made to a person other than the person in whose name the Certificate or Book-Entry Share so surrendered is registered, if such Certificate shall be properly endorsed or otherwise be in proper form for transfer (and accompanied by all documents reasonably required by the Exchange Agent) or such Book-Entry Share shall be properly transferred and the person requesting such payment shall pay any transfer or other Taxes required by reason of the payment to a person other than the registered holder of such Certificate or Book-Entry Share or establish to the reasonable satisfaction of Parent that such Tax has been paid or is not applicable. (f) Lost Certificates . If any Certificate shall have been lost, stolen or destroyed, then upon the making of an affidavit of that fact by the person claiming such Certificate to be lost, stolen or destroyed and, if required by Parent or the Exchange Agent, the posting by such person of a bond, in such reasonable amount as Parent may direct, as indemnity against any claim that may be made against it with respect to such Certificate, the Exchange Agent will issue in exchange for such lost, stolen or destroyed Certificate the Merger Consideration to which the holder thereof is entitled pursuant to this Article III . (g) No Further Ownership Rights in Company Capital Stock . All shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (together with cash in lieu of fractional shares of Parent Common Stock payable pursuant to Section 3.1(e) ) issued upon the surrender for exchange of Certificates or Book-Entry Shares in accordance with the terms of this Article III shall be deemed to have been issued (and paid) in full satisfaction of all rights pertaining to the shares of Company Capital Stock theretofore represented by such Certificates or Book-Entry Shares. After the Effective Time, there shall be no registration of transfers on the stock transfer books of the Company of shares of Company Capital Stock that were outstanding immediately prior to the Effective Time. If Certificates or Book-Entry Shares are presented to Parent, the Surviving Corporation, the Second Surviving Corporation or the Exchange Agent for transfer following the Effective Time, they shall be canceled against delivery of the applicable Merger Consideration for each share of Company Capital Stock formerly represented by such Certificates or Book-Entry Shares. (h) Termination of Exchange Fund . Any portion of the Exchange Fund which remains undistributed to the holders of the Certificates or Book-Entry Shares for one (1) year after the Effective Time shall be delivered to Parent or its designee upon demand, and any such holders prior to the Merger who have not theretofore complied with this Article III shall thereafter look only to Parent as general creditor thereof for payment of their claims for Merger Consideration and any dividends or distributions with respect to Parent Common Stock and Parent Series B Preferred Stock (if any) as contemplated by Section 3.2(d) . -11- (i) No Liability . None of Parent, the Merger Subs, the Company or the Exchange Agent, or any of their respective affiliates shall be liable to any person in respect of any shares of Parent Common Stock or Parent Series B Preferred Stock (or dividends or distributions with respect thereto) or cash held in the Exchange Fund delivered to a Governmental Entity pursuant to any applicable abandoned property, escheat or similar Applicable Law. If any Certificate shall not have been surrendered or Book-Entry Share not paid, in each case, in accordance with Section 3.2(c) , immediately prior to the date on which any Merger Consideration in respect of such Certificate or Book-Entry Share would otherwise escheat to or become the property of any Governmental Entity, any such Merger Consideration in respect of such Certificate or Book-Entry Share shall, to the extent permitted by Applicable Law, become the property of Parent free and clear of all claims or interest of any person previously entitled thereto. (j) Withholding Rights . Each of Parent, the Merger Subs, the Surviving Corporation and the Second Surviving Corporation and the Exchange Agent shall be entitled to deduct and withhold from any amounts otherwise payable pursuant to this Agreement to any person such amounts as are required to be deducted and withheld with respect to the making of such payment under the Code or any provision of applicable Tax Law. Any amounts so withheld shall be paid over to the applicable Governmental Entity and treated for all purposes of this Agreement as having been paid to the person in respect of which such deduction and withholding was made. Section 3.3. Certain Adjustments . Without limiting the other provisions of this Agreement, if at any time during the period between the execution of this Agreement and the Effective Time, any change in the number or type of outstanding shares of Parent Capital Stock or Company Capital Stock shall occur as a result of a reclassification, recapitalization, exchange, stock split (including a reverse stock split), combination or readjustment of shares or any stock dividend or stock distribution with a record date during such period, the Merger Consideration, the Exchange Ratio and any other similarly dependent items, as the case may be, shall be appropriately adjusted to provide the same economic effect as contemplated by this Agreement prior to such event; provided that nothing in this Section 3.3 shall be construed to permit any party to take any action that is otherwise prohibited or restricted by any other provision of this Agreement. Section 3.4. Further Assurances . If, at any time after the Effective Time, any further action is determined by Parent, the Surviving Corporation or the Second Surviving Corporation, as applicable, to be necessary or desirable to carry out the purposes of this Agreement or to vest the Surviving Corporation, the Second Surviving Corporation or Parent with full right, title and possession of and to all rights and property of the Merger Subs and the Company with respect to the Merger, the officers and directors of Parent, the Surviving Corporation and the Second Surviving Corporation shall be fully authorized (in the name of the Merger Subs, the Company, the Surviving Corporation, the Second Surviving Corporation and otherwise) to take such action. (c) Capital Structure . (i) The authorized capital stock of the Company consists of 990,000,000 shares of common stock, $0.001 par value per share (the “ Company Common Stock ”), and 10,000,000 shares of preferred stock, $0.001 par value per share (the “ Company Preferred Stock ”), 2,876,415 of which are designated as Series A Convertible Preferred Stock (the “ Company Series A Preferred Stock ”). As of 1:00 p.m. Pacific Time on October 29, 2026 (the “ Company Measurement Date ”), (A) 90,684,914 shares of Company Common Stock were issued -14- and outstanding (for the avoidance of doubt, excluding shares of Company Common Stock held by the Company in its treasury), (B) 0 shares of Company Common Stock were held by the Company in its treasury, (C) 2,876,415 shares of Company Series A Preferred Stock, and no other shares of Company Preferred Stock, were issued and outstanding, (D) 203,017 shares of Company Common Stock were subject to issuance pursuant to outstanding Company Options, (E) 1,565,041 shares of Company Common Stock were subject to issuance pursuant to outstanding Company RSU Awards, (F) 693,004 shares of Company Common Stock were subject to issuance pursuant to outstanding Company PSU Awards (assuming satisfaction of any performance vesting conditions at target levels), (G) 282,658 shares of Company Common Stock were reserved and available for issuance under the Company ESPP and (H) 7,271,887 shares of Company Common Stock subject to issuance upon conversion of the Company Convertible Notes. As of the date of this Agreement, each outstanding share of Company Series A Preferred Stock is convertible into one (1) share of Company Common Stock pursuant to the terms of the Company Certificate of Designation. (ii) All outstanding shares of Company Capital Stock are duly authorized, validly issued, fully paid and nonassessable and not subject to preemptive rights. (iv) As of the date of this Agreement, there are no stockholder agreements or voting trusts or other agreements or understandings to which the Company or any of its subsidiaries is a party with respect to the voting, or restricting the transfer, of the capital stock or other equity interests of the Company or its subsidiaries other than, in the case of the Company’s subsidiaries, the organizational documents of the Company’s subsidiaries. Except for matters that would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, (i) Parent and each of its subsidiaries are and have been since January 1, 2024 in compliance with all Environmental Laws, (ii) Parent and each of its subsidiaries have timely applied for, possessed and maintained all Parent Permits required under Environmental Law (“ Parent Environmental Permits ”), and all such Parent Environmental Permits are in good standing or, where applicable, a renewal application has been timely filed and is pending agency approval, and Parent and its subsidiaries are and since January 1, 2024 have been in compliance with all terms and conditions of the -44- Parent Environmental Permits, (iii) since January 1, 2024, none of Parent or any of its subsidiaries has received any written notice or other communication from any person that alleges that any of Parent or any of its subsidiaries is not in compliance with, or has any liability under, any Environmental Law, (iv) there has been no handling, treatment, storage, transportation, manufacture, marketing, sale, disposal or arranging for disposal of, Release, exposure of any person to, or ownership or operation of any property or facility contaminated by, any Hazardous Materials (or products or other items containing Hazardous Materials) so as to give rise to liability of Parent or any of its subsidiaries under Environmental Law, and (v) neither Parent nor any of its subsidiaries has assumed, undertaken, provided an indemnity with respect to, or otherwise become subject to, any liability of any other person under Environmental Law. (q) Real Property . (i) Except for matters that would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, Parent and each of its subsidiaries has good and marketable fee title (or the equivalent in any applicable foreign jurisdiction) to each and all real property owned by Parent or any of its subsidiaries (the “ Parent Owned Real Property ”), and good and valid leasehold title to all of real property leased, subleased or licensed, or otherwise used or occupied, by Parent or any of its subsidiaries, other than Parent Owned Real Property (the “ Parent Leased Real Property ” and, together with Parent Owned Real Property, the “ Parent Real Property ”), pursuant to leases with third parties which are enforceable in accordance with their terms, in each case subject only to Permitted Liens and the Enforceability Exceptions. Except as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, there are no existing (or to the knowledge of Parent, threatened in writing) eminent domain, condemnation, or similar taking proceedings with respect to any such Parent Real Property, or other Actions or proceedings that would reasonably be likely to cause Parent or its subsidiaries to curtail or modify its manufacturing and other operations at such Parent Real Property, or that would reasonably be expected to impair such operations, or any special assessments or improvements or activities of any public or quasi-public body which may give rise to any special assessment against any Parent Real Property. With respect to all such Parent Leased Real Property, Parent and each of its subsidiaries is in compliance in all material respects with the terms and conditions of each lease therefor, and neither Parent nor any of its subsidiaries has received any written notice of default thereunder which is outstanding and remains uncured beyond any applicable period of cure, except as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent. Except as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, Parent Real Property is in good operating condition and repair and is suitable for the conduct of the business as presently conducted therein. Except for as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, neither the operation of Parent nor any of its subsidiaries on Parent Real Property nor, to the knowledge of Parent, such Parent Real Property, violate any Applicable Law relating to such property or operations thereon. Except as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, neither Parent nor any of its subsidiaries could be required to expend more than $10,000,000 in causing any Parent Leased Real Property to comply with the surrender conditions set forth in the applicable lease and Parent expects to be able to continue to have the right to occupy Parent Leased Real Property through the remainder of the term of the applicable lease. Except as would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, neither Parent nor any of its subsidiaries has received any notice from any insurance company of any defects or inadequacies in any Parent Real Property or any part thereof which could materially and adversely affect the insurability of such Parent Real Property or the premiums for the insurance thereof or requesting the performance of any repairs, alterations or other work with which compliance has not been made. -45- (ii) Except for matters that would not have a Material Adverse Effect on Parent, (A) Parent and each of its subsidiaries has good and marketable fee title (or the equivalent in any applicable foreign jurisdiction) to each and all of its owned manufacturing equipment and other tangible property, and good and valid leasehold title to all of its leased manufacturing equipment and other tangible property pursuant to leases with third parties which are enforceable in accordance with their terms, in each case subject only to Permitted Liens and (B) such manufacturing equipment and other tangible property is in good operating condition and repair, ordinary wear and tear, and is sufficient for the uses in which such property is presently employed. (r) Voting Requirements . (i) The affirmative vote of the holders of a majority of the voting power of the shares of Parent Common Stock and Parent Series A Preferred Stock, voting together as a single class (with the shares of Parent Series A Preferred Stock voting on an as-converted basis), at the Parent Stockholders Meeting and entitled to vote thereon, is necessary to approve the Parent Share Issuance and (ii) the Parent Charter Amendment Approval is necessary to approve the Parent Charter Amendment (such approvals in clauses (i) and (ii) of this Section 4.2(r) , the “ Parent Stockholder Approvals ”). Other than the vote of each of the sole stockholders of Merger Sub I and Merger Sub II contemplated by Section 6.17 , the Parent Stockholder Approvals are the only vote of holders of any securities of Parent or the Merger Subs necessary to approve the Parent Share Issuance and the Parent Charter Amendment. (s) Opinions of Financial Advisors . (i) The Board of Directors of Parent has received the opinion of its financial advisor, Qatalyst Partners LP (“ Qatalyst ”), to the effect that, as of the date of such opinion, based upon and subject to the assumptions, limitations, qualifications and other matters set forth therein, the Exchange Ratio to be paid by Parent pursuant to, and in accordance with, the terms of this Agreement is fair, from a financial point of view, to Parent (it being agreed that such opinion is for the benefit of the Board of Directors of Parent and, for the avoidance of doubt, may not be relied upon by the Company or any of its affiliates). (ii) The Board of Directors of Parent has received the opinion of J.P. Morgan Securities LLC (“ J.P. Morgan ” and together with Qatalyst, the “ Parent Financial Advisors ”), to the effect that, as of the date of such opinion and based upon and subject to the assumptions made, procedures followed, matters considered and limitations on the review undertaken by J.P. Morgan in preparing its opinion as set forth in the opinion, the Exchange Ratio in the Merger is fair, from a financial point of view, to Parent (it being agreed that such opinion is for the benefit of the Board of Directors of Parent and, for the avoidance of doubt, may not be relied upon by the Company or any of its affiliates). (t) Brokers . Except for fees payable to the Parent Financial Advisor, no broker, investment banker, financial advisor or other person is entitled to any broker’s, finder’s, financial advisor’s or other similar fee or commission in connection with the transactions contemplated by this Agreement based upon arrangements made by or on behalf of Parent. (u) Merger Subs . (i) All of the issued and outstanding capital stock of Merger Sub I is, and at the Effective Time will be, owned directly by Parent, and Merger Sub I is, and at the Effective Time will be, treated, for U.S. federal income Tax purposes, as a direct wholly owned subsidiary of Parent. Merger Sub I was formed solely for the purpose of entering into the transactions contemplated by this Agreement and, since the date of its incorporation, has not carried on any business, other than the execution of this Agreement, the performance of its obligations hereunder and matters ancillary thereto. Merger Sub I has all requisite corporate power and authority to -46- enter into this Agreement and, subject to the adoption of this Agreement by the sole stockholder of Merger Sub I, to consummate the transactions contemplated hereby. The execution and delivery of this Agreement by Merger Sub I and the consummation by Merger Sub I of the transactions contemplated hereby have been duly authorized by all necessary corporate action on the part of Merger Sub I, subject, in the case of the Merger, to the adoption of this Agreement by the sole stockholder of Merger Sub I. This Agreement has been duly executed and delivered by Merger Sub I and, assuming the due authorization, execution and delivery of this Agreement by the Company, constitutes the legal, valid and binding obligation of Merger Sub I, enforceable against Merger Sub I in accordance with its terms, except for the Enforceability Exceptions. (ii) All of the issued and outstanding capital stock of Merger Sub II is, and at the Effective Time will be, owned directly by Parent, and Merger Sub II is, and at the Effective Time will be, treated, for U.S. federal income Tax purposes, as a direct wholly owned subsidiary of Parent. Merger Sub II was formed solely for the purpose of entering into the transactions contemplated by this Agreement and, since the date of its incorporation, has not carried on any business, other than the execution of this Agreement, the performance of its obligations hereunder and matters ancillary thereto. Merger Sub II has all requisite corporate power and authority to enter into this Agreement and, subject to the adoption of this Agreement by the sole stockholder of Merger Sub II, to consummate the transactions contemplated hereby. The execution and delivery of this Agreement by Merger Sub II and the consummation by Merger Sub II of the transactions contemplated hereby have been duly authorized by all necessary corporate action on the part of Merger Sub II, subject, in the case of the Merger, to the adoption of this Agreement by the sole stockholder of Merger Sub II. This Agreement has been duly executed and delivered by Merger Sub II and, assuming the due authorization, execution and delivery of this Agreement by the Company, constitutes the legal, valid and binding obligation of Merger Sub II, enforceable against Merger Sub II in accordance with its terms, except for the Enforceability Exceptions. (v) No Other Representations . (i) Except for the representations and warranties made in this Section 4.2 or any certificate delivered pursuant to this Agreement, neither Parent nor the Merger Subs or any other person makes any express or implied representation or warranty with respect to Parent or its subsidiaries or their respective businesses, operations, assets, liabilities or conditions (financial or otherwise) in connection with this Agreement or the transactions contemplated hereby, and Parent and the Merger Subs hereby disclaim any such other representations or warranties. In particular, without limiting the foregoing disclaimer, except as expressly provided in this Section 4.2 or any certificate delivered pursuant to this Agreement, neither Parent nor the Merger Subs or any other person makes or has made any representation or warranty to the Company or any of its affiliates or representatives with respect to (A) any financial projection, forecast, estimate, budget or prospect information relating to Parent or any of its subsidiaries or their respective businesses or (B) any other oral or written information presented, delivered or made available to the Company or any of its affiliates or representatives in the course of their due diligence investigation of Parent or the Merger Subs, the negotiation of this Agreement or in the course of the transactions contemplated hereby. (ii) Notwithstanding anything contained in this Agreement to the contrary, Parent and the Merger Subs acknowledge and agree that neither the Company nor any other person has made or is making, and Parent and the Merger Subs expressly disclaim reliance upon, any representations, warranties or statements relating to the Company or its subsidiaries whatsoever, express or implied, beyond those expressly given by the Company in Section 4.1 or any certificate delivered pursuant to this Agreement, including any implied representation or -47- warranty as to the accuracy or completeness of any information regarding the Company furnished or made available to Parent or the Merger Subs or any of their representatives.
